## Counting rules

- One row equals one concrete deliverable or closure. Assertions, retries, source-review checkpoints and individual recipe records do not inflate the total.
- Overall completion = completed rows / all counted rows. In-progress, blocked and unverified rows remain incomplete; no partial credit.
- Completed audits and scoped native proofs have their exact limits shown. An unresolved implementation is a separate open task, never passed because an audit finished.
- The percentage measures task count, not hours or equal effort. A difficult space task can take longer than a small recipe repair.
- The current approved/requested scope is fixed in this inventory version. Additions, removals and status changes must be recorded in the change history; no silent denominator reset.
- Closeout requires every counted task complete or explicitly removed/deferred by the user, plus the final acceptance/delivery conditions. Merely documenting a blocker does not complete it.
